- **Not verified:** real screen reader, real phone, second device, offline reload, older build. All remain **NOT TESTED** in the report's owner checklist.
- AI-readiness assessment (report section 14): no new interface is recommended yet; `loadOverview` and the pure readers are already a small read-only context surface.

### Household Services S1 — providers and standing arrangements (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `7b8c33f`, Pages run #60; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S1-REPORT-2026-11-06.md`. Code: `src/modules/services/` (new) plus a 2-line registration in `src/modules/index.ts`; nothing else in `src/` changed. **Built:** module at `#/services` (overview, arrangement page `#/services/a/<id>`, provider page `#/services/providers/<contact>`); arrangement record (module `services`, kind `arrangement`, defensive reader, strict save-time validation, unknown fields preserved, no account/password/code/bank fields); provider = shared Contact via `findOrCreateContact` (no second contact for the same name) with a name snapshot; `ensureNextVisitTask` (re-reads live Tasks inside the transaction; one open visit Task; Task carries `servicesArrangement` and `servicesOccurrence` markers that the Task editor preserves, confirmed by test and in the browser); `scheduleHealth` (no-task, duplicate-open, rule-differs, stale-marker, off-cadence, moved-once) with seven previewed, stale-refusing fixes; Change schedule (move the current visit, or keep it and apply the new pattern after it); Pause, Resume, End, Replace provider (one transaction, all-or-nothing, Undo, H4-style shared-task warning, no duplicate successor); one-way asset linking (`serves`, from Services only, retired and removed assets handled); Search, Quick Add `hs`, record source `arrangement:<n>`. **Not built:** visits and "Done, log it" (S2), Finance (S3), Today card, Calendar layer, seasons, renewals, reverse Home & Assets UI (S4), AI. **Evidence:** automated (vitest, including real `SyncEngine` two-device and backup/restore), browser (pane database, desktop, 375 px, dark mode, invented data); real-device NOT done. Judgment calls 1–15 are listed in the S1 report and the backlog and await review.

### Household Services S2 — visits, scheduling and history (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `2b9f845`, Pages run #61; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S2-REPORT-2026-11-06.md`. Code: `src/modules/services/` only. New: `visitModel.ts`, `visits.ts`, `VisitDialogs.tsx`, `VisitList.tsx`, `VisitPage.tsx`, `todayCards.tsx` and three test files. Edited inside the module: arrangement, overview and provider pages, `views.ts`, `schedule.ts`, `data.ts`, `changes.ts` (Recorder exported; Undo also clears a visit's links), `quickAddDef.ts`, `records.ts`, `searchSource.ts`, routes, css. No file outside `src/modules/services/` changed in `src/`; no schema, dependency, sync or backup change.
- **Visit record** (module `services`, kind `visit`, `visit:<n>` refs): arrangement id or null (one-off), provider contact id plus name snapshot taken at the time, title, status `completed | skipped | cancelled`, `cancelledBy`, date (+ optional end date), notes, `chargedCents` + `chargeKind` (information only), originating `taskId`, `occurrence` (the cadence date the Task stood for), task title snapshot, document links (http/https only), asset links (`serves`, visit to asset). Defensive reader (an unknown, missing or malformed status reads as `unsupported` with the raw value kept: never service, skipped or cancelled, shown as "Needs review", written back unchanged by an ordinary edit), unknown fields preserved (S3 fields such as `payment`, `txnRefs` pass through untouched), strict validation (completed not in the future; skipped and cancelled may be future; cancelled never charged as service; whole non-negative cents; valid charge kind; real dates). No account, password, code or bank field. No delete.
- **Done, log it / Skip / Cancel** (`logVisitFromTask`): ONE Dexie transaction: the existing `completeTask`, the next Task brought back to the arrangement's cadence (the Task Tasks spawned is re-dated, keeping its id and links), the visit written. Refuses on a stale preview, an already-logged Task or a booking reminder. Rollback proven by injected failures. Undo toast.
- **Cadence-anchored next date** (`nextAfterVisit`, pure): fixed = first cadence date after both the occurrence dealt with and the visit date, not before today (`generateDates` via `nextCadenceDate`); repeat-after = Tasks' own `nextDueDate` from the service day. A one-time move never shifts later visits; the next Task's time is the arrangement's standing time. Proven with a Sunday move of an every-other-Friday visit (Tasks' own anchor gives a different date; the mutation test shows it).
- **Booking reminders vs visits:** Task marker `servicesPurpose: 'book'`; no marker = visit Task (every S1 Task). Booking Tasks are excluded from the schedule, health, logging and "unlogged" lists.
- **Finished, not yet logged** (`unloggedVisits`, read-only): finished visit Tasks with no visit; "Log this visit" keeps the real finished date, does not finish the Task again, and re-aligns a stale Tasks-made copy. Never automatic.
- **One-off and late visits** (`logStandaloneVisit`): provider + work + date; no arrangement or Task is created; contact reuse; identical-twin guard; ended arrangements accept an explicitly logged late visit. **History** on the arrangement page, provider page (by provider at the time) and overview; completed-only "last serviced"; visit page with edit (provider, arrangement, Task and occurrence are not editable). **Duplicates** (`duplicateGroups`): same Task, same occurrence, or same-day hand-logged; flagged, never merged or deleted; no resolution workflow (deferred, needs destructive operations). One-off saves carry an `operationId` (one per press: a repeat saves once); matching one-offs are a weak "look-alike" notice, never rejected and not counted on Today. **Undo** of visit actions refuses (writing nothing) if any record it touched changed since (`Undo.after` sealed inside the transaction); S1 lifecycle Undos are not guarded.
- **Today card** `services.attention` (counts only: finished-not-logged, possible duplicate visits, arrangements needing a schedule check; hidden when empty). **Quick Add** `hs visit <what> with <provider> [date] [$amount]` (preview first, Undo). **Search** finds visits by title and provider name only.
- **Not built (deliberately):** seasonal scheduling (deferred, fallback documented), any Finance reference or payment (S3), Home & Assets handoff and reverse UI (S4), duplicate-resolution workflow, visit deletion, dismissing an unlogged item, any Calendar event, any AI. **Evidence:** automated (vitest, real `SyncEngine` two-device scenarios, backup/restore, failure injection, five mutation checks), browser (invented data, desktop, 375 px, dark mode, Escape and focus); real-device, screen-reader and offline NOT done.


### Household Services S2.1 — lifecycle Undo and synchronization hardening (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `afa05cb`, Pages run #62; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S2.1-REPORT-2026-11-06.md`. Code: `src/modules/services/` only (new `undo.ts`; edited `changes.ts`, `data.ts`, `visits.ts`, `Dialogs.tsx`, `ArrangementForm.tsx`, `VisitDialogs.tsx`, `quickAddDef.ts`). No new table, migration, dependency, sync-policy or other-module change; schema still Dexie v15.
- **One Undo framework (`undo.ts`):** `Recorder` and `applyUndo` moved there (re-exported from `changes.ts`). `Recorder.seal` (last step, same transaction) records the whole stored row of every touched record as order-independent text (`fingerprint`: sorted keys, `deletedAt` missing = null, unknown fields kept; only the SET fields `extraContactIds`, `acknowledged`, `tagIds`, `contactIds` are order-insensitive) plus the links attached to each. `applyUndo` re-reads all of it in its own transaction before writing, refuses (`UndoRefused`, plain message) on any difference, then reverses only what the action did; a failing write rolls everything back.
- **Covered:** Pause, Resume, End, Replace provider, Change schedule, all seven schedule fixes, visit logging (Done/Skip/Cancel/late/one-off), adding an arrangement (form and Quick Add). Open visit Tasks the plan showed are guard-only (`watch`) even when the action leaves them in place. Undo removes only the links the action made (never all links of a record). A token with no recorded state is REFUSED (tokens are transient UI callbacks, never persisted). A contact the action created is removed only by Quick Add Undo: refused if that contact was edited since, kept if anything still refers to it. `contactIsReferenced` finds the contact id as a whole number anywhere in any live record, link, attachment or due item (conservative, shape-independent; follow-up verified against every real Contact shape in Assets, Projects, Tasks, Calendar and Services).
- **Timestamp finding:** `updatedAt` is `Date.now()` (ms), two writes can share it, sync keeps stamps and ties go to the other device's copy, so equal stamps with different content are possible. The guard compares content, not time. A deterministic test (rewrite with the stamp preserved) proves it.
- **Tests/evidence:** 76 new tests (11 operations x success and four intervening changes, collision, links, contacts, legacy refusal, injected write failure, real two-device `SyncEngine` scenarios); four mutation checks fail the tests as intended; browser-checked normal Undo, refused Undo, Replace provider Undo, 375 px dark mode, 44 px main action, no console errors. NOT tested: real devices, screen reader, offline.
- **Contact follow-up (same day):** the original key-name scan caught every shape in use today but missed string-form link ends and unknown shapes; replaced as above, 30 tests, real two-device sync cases. Report section 19.
- **Not built (deliberately):** S3, seasonal scheduling, duplicate-visit resolution, AI, new tables, sync redesign. A refusal uses the existing toast (about 4.5 s on screen; the message is long).

### Household Services S3 — advanced cadences and financial integration (COMMITTED `d1737c3`; DEPLOYED through the test fix `d5cd5f9`, Pages run #65 success; run #64 for `d1737c3` failed on a date-dependent test and is superseded; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S3-REPORT-2026-11-06.md`. **2,085 tests in 122 files (after the pre-commit corrections).** S3-A and S3-B acceptance criteria met (deferrals listed below).
- **Cadences:** every 2 weeks = existing weekly rule, interval 2, one weekday. Twice a month = `cadence.semi` (two slots) computed by the pure adapter `semimonthly.ts` through `cadenceBetween`/`nextCadenceDate`/`isCadenceDate`; its Task has no repeat (a monthly repeat would be untrue). Invariants: every month gives exactly two distinct dates inside that month (refused at save otherwise); the date is the occurrence marker; no holiday calendar; a Task finished outside Services is picked up by "Finished, not yet logged" and logging it makes the next Task.
- **Payments:** visit fields `payment` (derived), `txnRefs`, `paidManualCents`; arrangement `txnRefs`. One source per record; switching needs confirmation; "No payment recorded" is never "Unpaid"; a payment never changes status. Visits with no payment store no payment keys. Finance is only read.
- **Shared code:** `txnRef`, `txnLookup`, `costSource` in `src/lib`, `TxnRefs` in `src/ui`, old paths re-export; `spend()` generic over group labels. H3 tests unchanged.
- **Totals (`servicesSpend.ts`):** built on H3 `spend()`; each piece once; completed, fees, other kept apart; shared payments not divided; typical price = median of 3 or more clear, unshared completed-visit payments; Estimates (`expectedCharges.ts`) per-visit only, never prorated; neutral review notes. Services and Home & Assets totals are never summed (household union is S5).
- **Deferred:** arrangement-level typed amounts, holidays, estimate conversion for other bases, seasonal scheduling, Quick Add for twice monthly, S4 and S5 items.
- **Pre-commit corrections (2026-11-06, report section 27; 2,085 tests, 122 files):** (1) payments shared across different outcomes go to a `shared` line, not to the first record's outcome; classification uses the whole module; lines reconcile to the total. (2) Twice-monthly is stored as top-level `semiCadence` with `cadence: null` (written only through `toStored`); an older build sees no schedule and carries the field through; a deliberate older-build schedule wins. Pre-S3 code is kept verbatim in `src/modules/services/legacy/` for the test. (3) `TxnPicker`/`RefLine`/`CostSourceField` accept `hide`; "Show amounts" reveals temporarily. (4) `expectedWindow` takes a `ScheduleState` per arrangement: open Task counted once on its real date; later visits after both its date and the date it stood for; finished-not-logged never recounted. Supersedes the earlier S3 notes on older builds (judgment call 2) and estimate windows.

### Household Services S4 — cross-module workflows and contract dates (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `c906b9c`, Pages run #66 success; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S4-REPORT-2026-11-06.md` (final correction section 29). **2,194 tests in 129 files** (after the pre-commit corrections). **Pre-commit corrections (2026-11-06, ChatGPT review):** (1) the Calendar layer now ALSO shows a rolling 12-month view of expected recurring visits (`plannedVisits.ts`, `dueProvider.ts`): derived from the arrangement's cadence through the existing S3 adapter, read only, nothing stored, ids `services:visit-<arrangement>:<date>`; horizon = today through the same day 12 calendar months later, both ends included, clamped; starts AFTER the one open visit Task (the Tasks layer shows only that Task, so no duplicates), after any finished visit, never before today; paused, ended, on-demand and after-each-visit schedules produce none; a moved visit does not shift later dates. **Limitation:** a future one-off skip or cancellation cannot be recorded in advance (needs a stored "skip this date" list, proposed not built). (2) An account with no opening date (not made by the app; can arrive from a restored or hand-made backup) no longer crashes the budget and the Today attention card (`budget.ts`, `retirement.ts`; nothing invented, no calculation changed). No new table, migration, dependency, backend, sync or Finance change; schema Dexie v15; backup format unchanged. New code in `src/modules/services/`: `contract.ts`, `contractData.ts`, `dueProvider.ts`, `handoff.ts`, `assetLinks.ts`, `projectLinks.ts`, `S4Dialogs.tsx`, `CrossModuleUI.tsx`, `ProjectLinkCard.tsx`; `src/modules/projects/RelatedServices.tsx`. Small edits in Home & Assets (asset page card, `ServiceForm` handoff mode, `AssetWork` `?fromVisit=`, delete preview, `insertService` exported), Calendar (sixth layer), Projects page, and the Services pages.
- **Reverse view:** asset page card "Household services" (arrangements via `serves`; pointer only, never maintenance that happened).
- **Handoff (`handoff.ts`):** completed visits only; owner chooses the asset; prefilled existing form; nothing saved until Save; `saveHandoff` writes the service record and the `recorded-as` link (`visit:<id>` to `assets:service:<id>`) in ONE transaction, refusing a repeat for the same asset (`HandoffExistsError`); Finance `txnRefs` carried as the same payment, typed amount only via a separate unchecked warned box (the service layer also refuses an unconfirmed equal cost); notes and links copied only when safe; later edits are never synchronized (`handoffDivergence` shows differences); two devices recording the same visit keep both records and raise a review notice (`duplicateHandoffs`). Source shown by a chip from the link.
- **Move to replacement (`assetLinks.ts`):** preview, single transaction, stale checks, touches the arrangement's `updatedAt`, sealed Undo through a new additive `linkOps` on `undo.ts`; visits' links are never rewritten; ended arrangements are not offered it.
- **Projects (`projectLinks.ts`):** `involves` from an arrangement or visit to a Project; atomic, sealed Undo; Project page card; vendor relationship is NOT a link; no Project or Finance write.
- **Contract dates:** `renewalDate`, `noticeByDate` (optional, stored only when set), Contract dates dialog with soft warnings, Undo; Calendar layer id `9_000_000_000_000_106` ("Household Services", provider `services`, one marker per date, titles only, ended arrangements show none; the corrections add the 12-month expected visits to the same layer); Today lines (counts only, 30 days, same `contractDeadlines`); replace provider copies dates only when ticked. No renewal engine.
- **Provider page:** grouped history (current, ended and replaced, completed visits, skipped and cancelled, linked projects, vendor projects, documents).
- **Invariants added:** a `recorded-as` link is the only handoff identity and duplicate guard (visit plus asset); S5's `householdSpend` (the S0 `householdHomeSpend`) uses it (typed amounts on a linked pair counted once; Finance pieces unioned by `pieceKey`); the S4-time statement that no combined total existed is superseded by S5.
- **Not built in S4 (the household total and insights were built later in S5):** duplicate-visit resolution, seasonal scheduling, holidays, Bills and Subscriptions, automatic renewal or cancellation, convert-visit-to-Project, real-device checks. **Evidence:** vitest (81 new), real `SyncEngine` two-device and G1 backup round trip, 12 mutation checks, browser (invented data, desktop, 375 px dark); NOT verified: light mode of new sections at 375 px, Move and Contract dialogs at 375 px, keyboard Tab walk, screen reader. **Unrelated finding (since FIXED, report section 29.2):** the Today attention card failed for an account with no opening date.

### Household Services S5 — Overview, household spending, insights and final acceptance (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `6863ac2`, Pages run #67 success, verified 2026-11-07 at the start of B0; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S5-REPORT-2026-11-07.md`. **2,265 tests in 133 files** (71 new). Code: `src/modules/services/` only (new `householdSpend.ts`, `insights.ts`, `periods.ts`, `servicesOverview.ts`, `OverviewParts.tsx`; `OverviewPage.tsx` rebuilt; `views.ts` gained `loadSnapshotAndLinks`; `PaymentUI.tsx` lost only the old `OverviewMoney`). No schema, dependency, backup, sync, Finance, Tasks, Calendar, Projects or Home & Assets change.
- **Overview reader:** `loadServicesOverview(period, today)` (one `links` read, one bulk Finance read) feeds the pure `buildServicesOverview`; it asks `buildOverview` (Today's counts come from it), `contractDeadlines`, `plannedVisitDates`, `servicesSpend`, `expectedWindow`, `householdSpend` and `buildInsights`. Nothing stored; repeated loads change no row. Sections: Needs attention (one list), Upcoming services (open Task plus derived dates; nothing created), Agreements to review (90 days), arrangements (status and provider filters), Recent activity (logged visits), Recorded spending (period selector), Estimates (30 and 90 days plus 12 months), Things to know.
- **`householdSpend(input, lookup, period)`** is the S0 7.6 `householdHomeSpend`: a pure union over H3's `spend()` UNCHANGED. Finance pieces deduplicate by `pieceKey` across modules (whole covers parts globally); typed amounts are NEVER matched by value: only a valid `recorded-as` link makes a visit and its Home & Assets record(s) one payment event (a Finance reference anywhere in the event leaves every typed amount out; otherwise the visit's typed amount counts once and a different amount is flagged; decided on all data, dated by the counting claim). A missing partner means no pair. Claims end included, merged, covered or excluded; included claims equal the total; the difference from the two module totals is explained exactly. Never an allocation, never Project money (only counts pieces Finance also tags to a Project), labelled "recorded payments, each counted once", never "all household spending". The Home & Assets figure includes asset purchases (S0).
- **Insights** (`insights.ts`, deterministic, no model): trend, 12-month estimate beside recorded money, unusual cost, long gap, repeated skips, moving dates, optional unrecorded maintenance, provider breakdown where unambiguous. Honest about thin history; never infers a provider failed to appear.
- **Standing decisions preserved (S4 and S5):** exactly ONE actionable visit Task per arrangement (the open Task); the Calendar layer adds a rolling 12-month view of EXPECTED visits derived from the cadence (never stored, starting after the open Task, paused/ended/on-demand produce none; a future one-off skip cannot be pre-recorded). Household spending counts each Finance piece once by `pieceKey`, never merges typed amounts by value, and is conservative with stale Finance references (they count 0 and are listed; typed amounts on a linked job are left out whenever the job holds any Finance reference, even a stale one). Real-device, two-device, offline and screen-reader acceptance are DEFERRED, not done.
- **Invariants added:** the household total is computed on read and never stored; typed amounts are never merged by value; a shared payment is never divided; insights and attention text carry titles and dates only, with money only through a money function (Hide Amounts); the Overview's counts agree with Today's for shared categories.
- **Not built (scope):** Bills & Subscriptions, Family Management, Personal CRM, AI, automatic payment posting or renewal, a notification service, seasonal scheduling, general reporting, bulk duplicate resolution, a visual redesign. **Evidence:** vitest (real Dexie, real backup, real two-device `SyncEngine`), 26-scenario matrix plus 300 random worlds, 28 mutation checks (all caught after test gaps were closed), performance (operation counts), browser (invented data, desktop and 375 px, light and dark, Hide Amounts, keyboard arrows, dialog Escape). **NOT verified:** real phone, two physical devices, offline, real screen reader, measured contrast, an in-browser re-run of creating an arrangement, saving a visit, the handoff, Project linking and the Calendar view.

### Bills & Subscriptions B0 — architecture and planning (REVISION 2 APPROVED WITH IMPLEMENTATION SAFEGUARDS by ChatGPT, owner accepted A-1 to A-11; committed `8059e5f`, Pages run #68 success; the text below was written as a proposal and is kept as the design record)

Document: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B0-PLAN-2026-11-07.md`. No source, schema, dependency or test change. **Revision 2 (after ChatGPT's REVISION REQUIRED verdict; accepted decisions retained, not reopened):**
- **Finance status, exactly:** `Txn.status` (pending, cleared, reconciled) is owner-maintained (manual entries, Scheduled approvals and loan payments start pending; the Clear button, a bank-file import and the reconcile screen set cleared and reconciled); it does not establish bank settlement, receipt or failure. Bills shows Finance's own status as a mandatory chip, adds no settlement state, and never uses "settled" or "cleared by the bank". Six evidence kinds: a linked transaction (with its status), an owner-confirmed link (the mechanism), "paid outside Ledger", "paid in full" with no amount, and suggestions (never proof, never counted). Primary wording "Paid" plus an evidence line (alternative "Payment recorded", owner decision A-3).
- **Cycle identity:** an immutable period `slot` (`m<YYYY-MM>`, `s<YYYY-MM>a|b`, `w<Monday>`, `x<id>`) plus a separate editable due date, with a 12-rule normalization contract and duplicate and conflict detection that never auto-merges.
- **One refinement of an accepted decision:** the kind name `cycle` stays, but each record is **one append-only fact** (invoice, due-date, payment-link, paid-outside, paid-in-full, skip, credit, suggestion-reject) so two offline devices cannot overwrite each other's evidence. Reason: whole-record last-writer-wins plus a device-local conflict list capped at 50 can lose a payment link. Still no `merge.ts` change; the single-mutable-record-plus-union-rule is the last-resort fallback.
- **Status model:** timing (five mutually exclusive classes), amounts (owed, paid, remaining), evidence and attention are independent derivations; a fixed-precedence primary label; a stored declaration never silently overrides live Finance evidence (suspended with words on a stale link or reversal); a confirmed $0.00 invoice is satisfied without payment.
- **Cross-module totals:** S5's `householdSpend` is NOT changed. B5 adds a separate `recordedAcrossModules` function behind a golden-master gate (full S5 output on the matrix and 300 random worlds, then a behaviour-neutral `unionRecords` extraction), with a fallback of no union. Every displayed total is defined (commitments and recorded payments never on one line).
- **Loans (read in source):** Loans records a payment as two pending Finance entries (a transfer and an interest-plus-escrow expense), exposes a modelled schedule (principal and interest only; escrow and PMI are owner-typed constants) and nothing about actual tax or insurance disbursements; `buildSchedule` internals not read (UNVERIFIED). Default: exclusion with "See Loans", an explicit owner `paidThroughLoan` mark, and an optional, off-by-default, separately labelled modelled line.
- **Review list:** any obligation may go on the cancellation or cost-review list; `need` (essential or flexible) is only a suggested label; savings counts only owner-listed items.
- **B1 kept proportionate:** B1.0 is the date helper only, with a copy-and-sweep-test fallback; **no Undo extraction** (a small local guard in Bills); an explicit "B1 does not need" list (no facts, Finance reading, Calendar, Tasks or Today).
- **Offline repair:** every concurrent-edit scenario in the review has a stated outcome, a detection rule and an explicit, previewed, undoable repair; no silent merge.

**What the original Revision 1 proposed (still in force except where the list above says otherwise; all subject to review):**
- **Five things kept apart:** obligation (new `bills` module record), expected occurrence (**derived on demand; a small stored `cycle` record only when a fact is recorded**: an invoice, a paid confirmation, a skip), payment arrangement (autopay or manual; never proof of payment), actual payment (a **Finance transaction, referenced, never copied**), lifecycle (active, paused, ended, plus optional dated terms; "in trial", "cancellation requested" and "did it renew?" are derived labels, never stored statuses).
- **Finance scheduled items stay authoritative payment instructions** (account-bound posting templates that also carry income and transfers and feed the forecast, alerts, badge, Finance Calendar layer and the "everyday spending" exclusion). A bill may optionally point at one (`scheduledRuleIds`); nothing is converted, posted, edited or deleted. Bills never feeds the cash forecast.
- **Evidence rules:** paid only with a confirmed Finance link or an owner "paid outside Ledger"; match suggestions (tiered, deterministic) never count and are never stored until confirmed; autopay is "scheduled", never paid; **Overdue** only when an invoice was recorded and a manual-pay bill passed its date with no evidence, otherwise "Not confirmed paid".
- **Money:** reuses the shared `txnRef`/`costSource`/`txnLookup` code and H3 `spend()` unchanged; four amounts kept apart (expected, billed, scheduled, paid); a shared payment is never divided; *(Revision 2 supersedes the earlier "widen `householdSpend`" idea)* a three-module recorded-payments figure is proposed for B5 only, as a separate `recordedAcrossModules` function behind a golden-master gate; S5's `householdSpend` is not changed.
- **Dates:** one slot/date resolver (Services' `semimonthly.ts` moved to `src/lib` with a re-export, proven equal to `generateDates` on plain rules by an oracle test); tracking starts the day a bill is added (no backfilled "missed" cycles; Finance's `dueInstances` backlog behaviour is a noted hazard, not copied); schedule and price revisions are append-only so history is never re-derived.
- **Calendar and Tasks:** a new read-only layer `9_000_000_000_000_107` with a rolling 12 months derived, titles carry no amounts, suppression against the Tasks layer, the Finance layer (for a linked rule) and Services contract dates; Tasks only for the next manual-pay bill and for notice-by and trial-end dates, opt-in, none for autopay.
- **Sync/deletion:** no `merge.ts` change; *(Revision 2)* cycle records are one append-only fact each, so concurrent evidence is kept and disagreements are detected and repaired explicitly, never auto-merged; an obligation with history cannot be permanently deleted (End instead); orphans detected and repairable; a union rule for concurrent payment links is no longer needed and stays only as a last-resort fallback.
- **Privacy:** no account or card numbers, passwords or codes; providers are Contacts; Hide Amounts covers every figure; no amount in Calendar, Search or Today text.
- **Phases (proposal):** B1 registry and schedule definition (starting with one small spike: the date helper, with a copy-and-test fallback; no Undo extraction), B2 occurrences, Calendar, Tasks, Today and contract dates, B3 confirmed Finance links and totals, **B3.1** suggestions and the Finance scheduled-item pointer, B4 cancellation, renewal and price history, B5 Overview, insights, union and acceptance. Optional B3.2 (create a Finance scheduled item from a bill) only if approved.
- **Differences from earlier statements (flagged, not silently changed):** contract-date Calendar markers move from B4 to B2; B3 is split; a new link type `bills-for`; a new Calendar layer id `..._107`; (the `householdSpend` `Mod` widening was dropped in Revision 2); Quick Add prefix `bs` (Finance keeps `b`).
- **UNVERIFIED in B0:** single-slot use of `slotDate` at month edges, the body of `buildSchedule` (matters only for the optional Loans line), (the `services/undo.ts` separability question is closed: not extracted), an older-build carry-through test of `bills` rows, the Task editor's handling of the new marker keys.

### Bills & Subscriptions B5 — Household finances: commitments, expected payments and recorded spending across Bills, Household Services and Home & Assets (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `8e77af465ed8b086c4b44106324c0991ac88d8f6`, Pages run #74 success; not real-device tested; 2026-11-08)

*(Corrected in ST-S1: this section was written before the commit. B5 is committed and deployed; the final committed test count is **3,310 in 179 files**, not 3,304 in 178.)*

Report: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B5-REPORT-2026-11-08.md`. Gate verified first: B4 is COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED (`a89f1972260b55a65ed45c78c03f733d619b35ef` = `origin/main`, Pages run #73 `success`). Baseline 3,169 tests in 172 files (re-run). Final **3,304 passing tests in 178 files** (+135 in 6 new files).

- **Golden master first:** 33 hand-computed tests pin `spend()`, `householdSpend()`, `billsRecordedPayments()`, Bills cash timing and commitments and the Services expected-visit window on the UNMODIFIED readers, before any union code. **No existing spending reader, Finance file, Calendar, Tasks or Projects file, schema, sync, backup or dependency was edited.** Only `src/modules/index.ts` (one registration) and `src/modules/services/ArrangementPage.tsx` (one card) changed among existing source files.
- **Recorded spending across modules (`household/unionSpend.ts`, pure):** calls `householdSpend()` (Services + Home & Assets, including S4's `recorded-as`) and `billsRecordedPayments()` unchanged and merges their already-resolved pieces by `pieceKey` (a deviation from B0 12.5, which proposed extracting `unionRecords` from `householdSpend.ts`; composition gives the same totals with zero change to those files, and `b3-spend.test.ts` still pins that they never mention Bills). One payment linked from several modules counts once; a whole covers a part another module links; refunds subtract; typed amounts are never matched by value; a stale link counts nothing (the piece still counts through any record that validly links it). **Attribution policy:** the additive breakdown credits a piece to the one module whose records reach it, or to "Shared between modules", never by list order; each module's own total is shown non-additively with a reconciliation. Every figure is a sum of listed pieces and typed amounts; the figure is not called reconciled while anything unresolved affects it. Verified against an independent oracle over 300 seeded random households, each shuffled.
- **Commitments and expected payments (`household/commitments.ts`, pure):** 7, 30 and 90 days and twelve months; known / estimated / not known / in dispute; unknown and disputed are counts, never zero. Reuses the Bills billing-period reader (a test pins equality with `cashTiming`), `buildCommitments` (B4 prices and the confirmed cancellation cutoff) and Services' `expectedWindow`, over ONE window (Bills' own twelve months). A bill and arrangement linked by `bills-for` fold into one commitment only when each is the other's only link, both have rows, nothing is unknown or disputed and the totals are equal to the cent; otherwise both stay counted with an overlap notice. Never reads Finance Scheduled or the forecast. Home & Assets contributes none (its maintenance is Tasks with no expected cost).
- **UI:** module `household` at `#/household` (expected payments, upcoming contractual actions, commitments, recorded spending with a period choice, spending by household area with "why this is less than the module pages added together", needs review classed by kind, every counted payment with all associations, where things are managed); a read-only **Related bill** card on the Services arrangement page (the existing `bills-for` link; a missing or ended bill is explained, never linked by name); one counts-only Today card `household.attention` (only cross-module concerns). Hide Amounts masks every figure; a failed read shows an error and no totals.
- **Evidence:** real Dexie, the real two-device `SyncEngine`, real backup and restore, bulk-read counts (no per-row and no full transaction-table read), 300 bills / 3,000 transactions, damaged records, injected failure of the first and of the second Finance read, whole-database fingerprints before and after, source guards, static markup tests, 25 mutation checks (report section 20; two real test gaps found by them were repaired), browser acceptance at desktop and 375 px, light and dark, with real clicks and keyboard (invented data, isolated origin; real flaws found and fixed, report section 21). **Not verified:** a real phone, offline use, a second physical device, an older build, a screen reader, a real Tab walk of the whole page.

### Bills & Subscriptions B4 — subscription lifecycle, trials and renewals, cancellation evidence, price history and the Household Services link (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `a89f197`, Pages run #73 success, verified at the start of B5; the "uncommitted" wording below was written before the commit and is SUPERSEDED; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B4-REPORT-2026-11-07.md`. Gate verified first: B3.1 is COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED (`f2db2a782b8646a52fbf8b8d8620772c52df8e29` = `origin/main`, Pages run #72 `success`). Baseline 2,973 tests in 164 files (re-run). Final **3,169 passing tests in 172 files after the pre-commit correction** (3,141 in 170 before it) (baseline 2,973 in 164; +168 tests in 6 new test files).

- **Model:** lifecycle evidence is a new KIND of `bills` module record, `lifecycle` (B0 10.4's named escalation path; the same pattern as B3.1's `discovery`): `trial`, `trial-extend`, `trial-outcome`, `price-step`, `renewal`, `renewal-outcome`, `cancel-plan`, `cancel-request`, `cancel-confirm`, `cancel-withdraw`, `reactivate`, `service-link`, `ack`. Added, never edited; `replaces` (a later record corrects an earlier one; both stay visible), `targets` (what a withdrawal negates) and `seen` (the ids a device could see, so offline devices that did not see each other are told apart from one owner correcting themselves). Unknown fields are carried; unknown or damaged records are listed, counted for nothing, never rewritten. No table, migration, dependency, sync rule or backup change (Dexie 15, 40 tables, pinned by a test); one backup-summary label.
- **Derived, never stored:** `lifecycleView` (attached to each obligation by the loaders as the reader-only `data.lc`; `toStored` drops it) gives the effective records, effective price steps, the trial and renewal in force, the cancellation in force, conflicts and the confirmed cutoff date. States are derived and several can hold at once. A confirmed, undisputed effective date stops EXPECTED bills (`scheduleSource`) without ever changing the stored status.
- **Invariants held (each a test):** a request is not a confirmation; the effective date, not the confirmation date, decides; an unknown price is never zero; a new price never touches a recorded invoice, payment link or Finance row; a passed date never means anything happened; disagreeing records (two effective dates, two prices for a day, overlapping prices, two renewal dates, a withdrawal beside an unseen confirmation) all survive on both devices and the disputed value is not used until the owner chooses; Finance, Scheduled rules, payment links and Calendar events are never written; Tasks are made only by `tasks.ts`; an action is previewed, refuses a stale preview, runs in one transaction, rolls back whole, and has a guarded Undo.
- **Surfaces:** a Subscription life card on the bill page (states, price and next change, trial, renewal, cancel-by, cancellation, price history, History timeline, disagreements, charges after a cancellation, a possible price change from Finance, the Household Services link); dialogs for trial, extension, outcome, price, renewal, "Did it renew?", cancellation request, confirmation, withdrawal, reactivation, resolve; four new Needs-review/Today categories (cancellation awaiting confirmation after 7 days, records that disagree, possible charges after cancellation, a Finance Scheduled payment still active for a cancelled or ended bill); Calendar markers for renewal (cadence repeated inside the rolling 12 months, derived), cancellation effective date and price change; Tasks for cancel-by, trial end, a manual renewal and one optional follow-up.
- **Household Services:** the optional `bills-for` link (a plain shared link; owner-confirmed preview, audit record, guarded Undo; `crossLinks.ts` is the only writer, `undo.ts` can restore one). The Calendar leaves out a Bills cancel-by or renewal marker only for the SAME kind of date on the SAME day and only while the Services marker is shown. Shown on the Bills page only; Services pages, `householdSpend()` and Bills recorded payments are untouched.
- **Deviations to review:** own record kind instead of embedded fields; cancellation never changes status; a trial ending on the cancel-by day is one Task (B2 made two); two new Task purposes (`renewal`, `followup`); `undo.ts` `linksRemoved`; one allow-list string in `b31-guards.test.ts`; the old guards' text patterns do not match `repo<Link>(d.links)`, so `b4-guards.test.ts` states the real link-writer rule; the Services marker id format is written out in Bills and pinned by a test (Bills must not import Services).
- **Not built (scope):** B5; B3.2; a Services-page card for the link; B0's monthly-to-annual workflow, replace/migrate successor and Project `involves` link; any automatic cancellation, renewal, conversion, price change, link or Finance write. **Real-device, real-older-build, screen-reader and Tab-walk acceptance NOT done.**
- **PRE-COMMIT CORRECTION (ChatGPT: APPROVED WITH FOLLOW-UPS, DO NOT COMMIT YET; done 2026-11-08; still uncommitted):** report section 25. (1) The optional "also mark ended" action on a cancellation confirmation is REMOVED (it made a future-dated cancellation read Ended immediately). A confirmation records evidence only and never changes the stored status; the effective date decides what is expected (the effective day itself is covered and reads "effective"); the owner ends the bill with the existing End workflow ("Mark it ended..." is offered once the date has arrived). The Finance reads for the after-cancellation checks no longer use the real clock. (2) Shared-link guard: `assertBillsLink` in the single writer (`crossLinks.ts`), a behavioural audit of every live link touching Bills (only Task `about` and `bills-for` exist), negative tests, and a targeted restriction on which files may reference the links table (only five; only `crossLinks.ts` and `undo.ts` may wrap it). (3) B2/B4 deadline overlap verified by ten tests; no code change. Totals 3,169 tests in 172 files; 30 of 30 mutation checks caught; decision O-14 withdrawn. Test config: the default `testTimeout` was raised to 30 s in `vite.config.ts` because heavy sync tests could exceed 5 s under full-suite load (no assertion changed).
- **Older (B3.1) build:** keeps lifecycle records untouched through sync, backup and restore and never converts them; it does not know a confirmed cancellation date (keeps showing expected bills after it until updated) and does not draw the new markers. Nothing is lost.


### Bills & Subscriptions B3.1 — discovery, payment-match suggestions, the Finance Scheduled link and Calendar coordination (BUILT AND TESTED LOCALLY, 2026-11-07; UNCOMMITTED, NOT STAGED, NOT PUSHED, NOT DEPLOYED; not real-device tested; awaiting independent review)

Report: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B3.1-REPORT-2026-11-07.md`. **2,973 tests in 164 files** (B3 baseline 2,768 in 155, re-run at the start). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` succeeds, schema still Dexie v15 with 40 tables (pinned), no table, migration, dependency, backend, sync or backup-format change. **Governing rule: Ledger may discover, suggest and explain. Only the owner may confirm relationships or actions.** Nothing here ever creates, edits, clears, posts or skips anything in Finance (byte-for-byte snapshots around every operation); `spend()`, `txnRef`, `costSource`, `txnLookup`, the shared `TxnPicker`, `householdSpend`, `lib/rules.ts`, `lib/schedule.ts` and the Tasks engine are NOT modified.
- **Gate (verified, not assumed):** `main` = `origin/main` = `ceb0ea8` (B3), Pages run #71 `success` (first seen `in_progress`; no code touched until it completed), tree clean, baseline 2,768 in 155 files.
- **One read-only Finance loader (`src/lib/financeRead.ts`):** `loadAllTxns`, `loadTxnsBetween`, `loadScheduled`; re-exports only the pure `normPayee`, `detectRecurring`, `occurrencesBetween`. The B3 guards (no Bills file mentions `.txns`, `.recurring`, `.recurringLog`, `.loans` or imports the Finance files that contain writers) are UNCHANGED and pass over every new file; `b31-guards.test.ts` pins the loader (no write, no posting, no network, exact exports, exact importers), pure readers, who may write which record, no schema, dependency, percentage or settlement wording.
- **Discovery (`discovery.ts`, pure):** `cleaned()` adapter in front of the UNCHANGED detector (money out only, no transfers, setup, loans, duplicates once, accounts merged); tiers derived from observable facts (never a percentage); two payments only = "Needs more information" with no frequency claimed; never income, transfers, loan payments, irregular sets or a payee matching an ENDED bill (counted). Finds the bill that covers a payee (word-based `payeeMatch.ts`), similar names, Scheduled rules that may stand for it ("a plan"), and the owner's choices. UI: `DiscoverCard.tsx` on the Overview; Review writes nothing; four distinct choices (Create a bill from this suggestion = the existing form prefilled via `formSeed.ts`, Link to an existing bill, Not a recurring expense, Dismiss for now).
- **Choices (`discoveryData.ts`):** `bills` module records of kind `discovery` (one per choice, newest wins, no transaction ids or payment lists, backup label "Recurring-expense review choices"). Dismiss for now = 60 days; materially new = cadence change, double the evidence plus three payments, or a fixed amount moved by over 25%. `createFromSeed` makes the bill, its Contact, the "tracked" record and the validated rule link in ONE transaction (repeat press reuses the first bill); `rememberOnBill` adds a payee word only with consent.
- **Suggestions (`suggest.ts`, pure; `suggestData.ts` loads in bulk):** up to three, two tiers, evidence and counter-evidence in words (amounts through Hide Amounts), B0 date windows, amount only when reliable, linked-rule posting from the posted log only (never "the date passed"), whole and part pieces, refunds noted against, transfers and setup never, already-linked-elsewhere (this bill, another bill, Household Services, Home & Assets via the shared `readTxnRefs`) shown separately and qualified. Rendered by `SuggestionsUI.tsx` ABOVE the picker (no picker change); a suggestion-sourced pick is re-checked with `suggestionStale` against a fresh read just before `recordFact`.
- **`suggestion-reject` fact:** `{ obligationId, slot, fact, ref: TxnRef }`; period-scoped; idempotent; added or removed, never edited; no Task; guarded Undo; "Look at it again"; a changed or reordered rejected piece returns only as "possible" with a warning; an older (B3) build carries it through as an unknown fact.
- **Finance Scheduled link (`scheduledLink.ts`, `scheduledLinkData.ts`, `ScheduledUI.tsx`):** `scheduledRuleIds`; spending rules only (income, transfers, missing, held by another bill in force, ended bill, loan bill refused with a reason); preview then confirm; removable; read-only panel; drift notices (explained = information, unexplained = "worth a look"; the bill's next date is compared with the rule's nearest occurrence, approved or not: a false alarm found by mutation testing was fixed); two offline devices linking one rule to two bills keep both and list it under Needs review (`ruleShared`, counted on Today and the Overview) with an explicit "keep it on this bill"; a deleted or disabled rule keeps the bill, never changes payment status, never recreates a rule; prefill from a rule (B0 mapping; an unfaithful recurrence explained, never substituted).
- **Calendar coordination:** `DerivedItem.coveredBy` (set by the Bills provider only for a linked live spending rule, the same date, no log entry, no unresolved drift) and `dropCovered` (hides only while the covering item is itself shown; Finance's approval marker never hidden; ids and phone-calendar ids unchanged; nothing stored). Household Services markers are NOT coordinated (needs B4's `bills-for` link).
- **Checks:** real Dexie, real two-device `SyncEngine` (10 scenarios), real backup and restore, injected-failure rollbacks, double-submit idempotency, Finance non-mutation snapshots, bulk-read and speed tests (about 6,000 payments scanned in about half a second including seeding), source guards, 66 mutation checks (65 caught, 1 equivalent; eight first-pass survivors closed), browser checks at desktop and 375 px, light and dark, Hide Amounts, Escape and focus (all DOM-level: the pane was hidden, so no screenshots and no real pointer or Tab walk).
- **Not built (by scope):** B3.2 (create a Finance Scheduled item from a bill), B4, B5, any automatic link, bill, payment or Finance write, Household Services marker coordination. **Not verified:** real phone, two physical devices, offline use, a real older build, a real screen reader, a real keyboard Tab walk, screenshots in either theme, custom palettes, the .ics file in a phone calendar.

### Bills & Subscriptions B3 — Finance payment links, with B3.0A (Household Services Upcoming window) and B3.0B (Calendar Select all / Deselect all, Finance Hide Amounts) (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `ceb0ea8`, Pages run #71 success, verified at the start of B3.1; the "uncommitted" wording below was written before the commit and is SUPERSEDED; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B3-REPORT-2026-11-07.md`. **2,761 tests in 154 files** (B2 baseline 2,609 in 147, re-run at the start). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` succeeds, schema still Dexie v15 with 40 tables (pinned by a test), no table, migration, dependency, backend, sync or backup change; `spend()`, `txnRef`, `costSource`, `txnLookup`, the shared `TxnPicker`, `householdSpend` and the Tasks engine are NOT modified.
- **Gate (verified, not assumed):** `main` = `origin/main` = `aca2208` (B2), Pages run #70 `success`, tree clean, baseline 2,609 in 147 files.
- **B3.0A:** `services/upcomingWindow.ts` (7, 14, 30, 90; default 30; `services-upcoming-days` per device, invalid or unavailable storage gives 30). `buildServicesOverview` takes `upcomingDays` and returns EVERY visit in the window (inclusive of the date N days away; an overdue open visit Task is always listed, a far one is not); the card shows 10 rows, says "Showing 10 of N" and offers Show all; provider filter before the row limit; Overview list only (the 12-month Calendar layer, the one-Task rule, recurrence, visits and payments untouched; no writes). The S5 test that assumed a fixed 60 days now assumes the 30-day default (intended change).
- **B3.0B:** the one existing preference `calendar-hidden-layers`, read by the pure `calendar/layerVisibility.ts`: either the old list of layers that are off (also what "everything on" is: `[]`) or `{"only":[ids]}` ("Deselect all" is only-with-no-ids). A new layer is on only while everything is on; Personal-only survives new modules; switching everything on returns to "everything on"; deleted layers ignored. Legend has Select all / Deselect all and "N of M calendars shown"; an empty Calendar is explained. Today does not read it; .ics export offers the visible own calendars as before. Not in `PREFERENCE_KEYS` (like the Calendar's other view preferences). **Finance Hide Amounts:** `ledgerDueItems(d, q, hide)` masks Finance Calendar text (scheduled item = payee only; other amounts masked); the .ics keeps full text via optional `DerivedItem.icsTitle` / `icsDetail` (so a screen preference never silently changes an exported file; export dialog warns; open owner decision O-2). Home "Coming up" not covered.
- **The `payment-link` fact:** one `cycle` fact = one shared `TxnRef` (reference + snapshot + split fingerprint); added or removed, never edited; no copy of money; older builds read it as unknown and carry it through. `readCycle` rejects a missing or junk reference as unreadable (kept, never counted).
- **Money (all live, nothing stored):** `payments.ts` calls the UNCHANGED `spend()` with one record per period (each `pieceKey` once, whole covers parts, missing / split-changed / not-spending count 0 and are KEPT, refunds lower the net) and adds link roles (counted, duplicate, covered, review), Finance's own status of each counted piece (pending, cleared, reconciled: owner marks and imports, never "settled"), and the reversal test. `amounts.ts`: ONE source per period: any effective link makes Finance the source (typed amounts then left out of every figure and listed, even if every link is stale); else the single typed amount. `labels.ts`: `marked-paid-short` (explicit "paid in full" declaration, Finance shows less: the owner's statement is respected and the difference stated, Finance source only so B2 typed behaviour is unchanged), suspended declarations (C1, C2) read "Needs review", autopay now "not yet seen in Finance".
- **Workflow and writes (`cycleData.ts`, `paymentLinks.ts`, `PaymentUI.tsx`):** Link a Finance payment (shared `TxnPicker` with `hide`; preview via `planFact` that runs the same checks as saving and shows the resulting label in words; optional finish of the reminder Task), Replace this payment (one transaction), Remove link (previewed, soft delete, guarded Undo), Replace the typed payment with a Finance payment (explicit `switchTyped`; the typed record gets `supersededBy` the link, never erased; Undo restores). Refused in words: the same piece twice, whole beside its part, deleted / transfer / setup / changed-split transaction, loan-tracked bill, a typed amount over links. Every write is one transaction with a stale guard and the guarded Undo; fault injection proves rollback.
- **Finance is only READ, before the write transaction opens:** `tasks.ts` `financeLookupFor` / `inBillsTx` (one bulk `loadTxnMap`), `readModel.ts` `loadBillsSnapshot` (`financeTxns`); `periodsOf` / `derivePeriod` / `planningPeriods` take the lookup (a guard test fails any production call that omits it: a forgotten one would read every payment as missing). No Bills file touches `d.txns`; no Finance table is written (byte-for-byte snapshot tests around link, replace, remove, switch, repair, move, Undo; the transaction's status is never changed).
- **Conflicts and Today:** new kinds `typed-and-finance` (repair: Use this Finance payment, typed set aside), `payment-duplicate`, `payment-overlap`, `payment-shared` (flagged, "leave as is" only). New attention categories `paymentReview` (scanned over EVERY recorded period), `paymentChanged`, `partlyPaid` (only when due soon, today or late), `typedFinance`, `duplicatePieces`; Today counts only (no name, no amount; tested). A Finance-paid period is not an open Calendar marker; no recurring event stored.
- **Tasks:** `tasks.ts` still the only writer (existing Tasks API). Paying a period offers to finish its reminder and ensures the next (one open pay Task). Removing the only payment ensures one reminder for the reopened period; the later reminder is REPORTED, not deleted. A Finance change after payment is reported and silently creates and deletes no Task. Reads write nothing.
- **Payment history:** read-only card on every bill (periods newest first; billed, linked Finance payments with live amounts and status words and a link into Finance, typed notes, credits "not a payment", collapsed "Set aside, not counted", review notes, what is still owed). No separate payment ledger.
- **B5 reader:** `billsSpend.ts` (`billsCostRecords` → shared `spend()` → `billsRecordedPayments`, `explain`): each Finance piece once across periods and bills, shared pieces flagged, typed only for a period with no link, loans and unknown bills left out, explainable piece by piece. `householdSpend` NOT changed or widened (a test pins that its source does not mention Bills).
- **Evidence:** vitest including real Dexie, real two-device `SyncEngine` (11 scenarios incl. typed-vs-Finance, same piece on both devices, deleted / altered / reordered Finance data, end vs link, move, guarded Undo, unknown fact survival), real backup and restore, atomic-write fault injection, 300 seeded random worlds and a 216-case matrix (reconciled to `spend()`), performance (one bulk read per load, none per period), malformed data, source guards, mutation checks (report section 17), browser checks of journeys A to F in the in-app pane at desktop width, 375 px, light and dark (report section 18; the pane was HIDDEN, so these were DOM-level interactions read back from the page: no screenshots, no real pointer or Tab walk). Journey F found and fixed a real defect: the Needs-review page had no group for the five new payment categories. **NOT verified:** real phone, two physical devices, offline, a real screen reader, a visual (screenshot) review, a real keyboard Tab walk, a real older build, Week / Work-week views visually and the .ics file inside a phone calendar. (Superseded: the 3.6:1 chip contrast figure was a measurement error; chips pass 4.5:1.)
- **Pre-commit follow-up (report section 23, 2026-11-07, uncommitted):** real pointer and keyboard review done (pane drawable); fixed shared `Modal` focus restoration (opener now read at first render; Escape returns focus to the opening button); chip contrast re-measured with computed colours and found fine (4.72 to 5.15 light, 5.56 to 8.59 dark; the earlier 3.6:1 was a measurement error, no CSS change); Home "Coming up" now masks Finance amounts under Hide Amounts (.ics download unchanged); new `declaredShort` category lists "marked paid in full, Finance shows less" on Needs Review, Today and the Overview (declaration kept, real Finance amount shown, nothing added). +7 tests. Financial architecture unchanged. Decisions now: O-1 mixed cash plus Finance DEFERRED; O-2 export amounts RETAINED with warning, separate control DEFERRED; O-3 RESOLVED; O-4 RESOLVED as the default; O-5 chosen-set behaviour APPROVED.
- **Open owner decisions (original list; see the line above for their status):** O-1 one bill paid partly in Finance and partly in cash (the one-source rule cannot represent it; recommendation: keep it for now); O-2 exported calendar files and Hide Amounts (a separate "leave amounts out" choice?); O-3 Home "Coming up" and Hide Amounts; O-4 "declared paid in full, Finance shows less" as "Partly paid" instead; O-5 hiding ONE calendar by hand makes later new calendars start off (literal reading of the brief).
- **Not built (scope):** B3.1 (suggestions, matching, scheduled-item pointer, cross-layer suppression), B4, B5, any automatic matching or posting, any Finance write.

### Bills & Subscriptions B2 — billing occurrences, due dates, Tasks, Calendar, Today and manual billing facts (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `aca2208`, Pages run #70 success, verified at the start of B3; the "uncommitted" wording below was written before the commit and is SUPERSEDED; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B2-REPORT-2026-11-07.md`. **2,609 tests in 147 files** (B1 baseline 2,463 in 142, re-run at the start; 130 new). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` succeeds, `git diff --check` clean (line-ending notes only), schema still Dexie v15, no table, no migration, no dependency, no backend, no change to `db.ts`, `merge.ts`, `backup.ts`, `src/types.ts`, the sync engine, Finance, Projects, Home & Assets or Household Services behaviour. Outside the Bills folder only: `calendar/model.ts` (one constant), `calendar/data.ts` (one seed row), and two existing tests that counted the built-in Calendar layers (`calendar.test.ts`, `sync/sync.test.ts`: six became seven).
- **Gate (verified, not assumed):** `main` = `origin/main` = `3bcf3a3` (B1), Pages run #69 `success`, tree clean, baseline 2,463 in 142 files.
- **Model:** each `cycle` record is ONE FACT about one obligation and one immutable period slot (`m<YYYY-MM>`, `s<YYYY-MM>a|b`, `w<Monday>`, `x<id>`). Fact types: `invoice`, `due-date`, `paid-outside`, `paid-in-full`, `skip`, `credit`. `payment-link` and `suggestion-reject` are B3/B3.1 and are read as unknown (kept, shown as needing review, never counted). Facts are only added (or edited/removed one record at a time), so two offline devices cannot overwrite each other's evidence and **no sync or merge change was needed**.
- **Derived, never stored:** period, timing (five mutually exclusive classes), amounts (owed, paid, remaining; unknown never zero; two different invoice amounts leave owed unknown), the primary label (fixed precedence; no settlement words; autopay never "paid" or "overdue"; "Overdue" needs a recorded invoice), cash timing (7/30/90 days: known, estimated, unknown; not a payment, not fed to the Finance forecast), attention counts (one list behind the Overview, Needs review and the Today card).
- **Conflicts and repairs:** `findConflicts` reports contradictory evidence and look-alikes (two invoices, two due dates, skip beside a payment, two typed payments, duplicates, one document number on two periods, look-alike extras, records from an earlier schedule, records arriving at a vacated period, records of a missing bill, unreadable records) and resolves NOTHING. Repairs (Use this one, These are different bills, Leave as is, Remove for orphans) preview first, re-detect inside the transaction (stale refused), write atomically, set aside rather than delete, and have a guarded Undo. "Move to another billing period" previews every record, keeps amounts/payments/due dates unchanged, writes `movedFrom`, refuses an occupied target, an unknown period and a period holding an unreadable record, and is guarded and undoable.
- **Schedule changes with facts:** `planScheduleChange` now takes the slots that hold facts; a period with a fact counts as applied (the B1 carry-forward). A change that would put a new schedule's bill in a period that already holds information is refused with the period named.
- **Tasks:** `tasks.ts` is the only writer, through the existing Tasks API; markers `billsObligation`, `billsCycle`, `billsPurpose`, `billsDue`, `billsDeadline`, `billsDoOn`; link `about`. A Task exists only when the owner turned reminders on (a bill with no setting gets none); one open pay Task per bill; none for autopay unless asked; deadline Tasks for cancel-by and trial end; a Task the owner touched or finished is never changed or deleted; pausing or ending removes (soft delete, restorable, guarded Undo) only an untouched Bills-made Task for something the bill no longer expects; duplicates and stale Tasks are reported ("Update reminders" on Needs review cleans the untouched ones). The Task date is a reminder date and the due date is shown separately. Finishing a Task is NOT payment: "Done, mark paid" writes the note, finishes the Task and makes the next one in one transaction with Undo; a Task finished elsewhere is "finished, not yet confirmed".
- **Calendar and Today:** layer `9_000_000_000_000_107`, derived on demand, 12-month horizon, titles carry no amount; a Task on the same day for the same period stands for the marker, a Task on another day does not (purpose and occurrence, not title). Today card `bills.attention`: counts only, hidden when empty.
- **Deliberately NOT built (scope):** Finance reading or links, payment matching and suggestions, the Finance scheduled-item pointer, B3.1 cross-layer suppression, cancellation/renewal workflows (dates only; nothing renews or cancels), price-step editing, cross-module totals, "activity after the end date" notice, re-homing orphan records, edit of a reminder Task's lead in the Task itself.
- **Pre-commit corrections (after ChatGPT's "approved with follow-ups", report section 21):** (1) a paused or ended bill: untouched Bills-made reminders for something no longer expected are removed (soft delete through the Tasks API, guarded Undo, previewed); a Task the owner touched or finished is never changed and a touched open one is reported; resume makes one new reminder; "Update reminders" on Needs review cleans strays after a sync; (2) Task association = markers on the Task row (how Bills finds Tasks) plus one `about` link written by `createTask`; a dangling-link bug (a deleted Task's link blocked deleting a bill with no history) was fixed; the "no shared link is written" wording was corrected; (3) the "no deadline reminders" choice moved from inside `reminders` to the top-level `noDeadlineTasks` so a B1 build editing the bill keeps it (simulated against the B1 rule, not run on a real B1 build); (4) direct Calendar month, December and 375 px agenda acceptance in an emptied, re-seeded pane database (layer chip, corrected due date moves the marker with a stable period id and phone-calendar id, Task on another date does not hide the marker, a same-date Task suppresses only its duplicate, notice/trial/renewal, layer toggle, Finance and Services unaffected, no amount in any Bills marker). **2,609 tests in 147 files.**
- **B3 SCOPE RECORDED (owner request, NOT built, NOT authorized yet):** **B3.0A** Household Services Upcoming filter: windows 7, 14, 30 and 90 days, default 30, persisted, Overview visibility only (not the 12-month Calendar horizon, not the one-actionable-Task rule). **B3.0B** Calendar visibility controls: Select All and Deselect All; all calendars visible by default for a fresh configuration; manually selecting only Personal afterwards; explicit selections persisted; sensible behaviour when new Calendar layers are registered; no event or recurrence data mutated. **B3 carry-forward (not built, not authorized):** B3.0A and B3.0B as above; **B3 Finance integration** (explicit Finance transaction and split linking through the existing `txnRef`/`costSource`/`spend()` utilities, pending/cleared/reconciled status labels, typed-versus-linked payment precedence, partial payments, refunds and reversals, stale references, no double counting, no Finance writes); **known existing Finance issue seen during B2 Calendar acceptance, NOT fixed:** the Finance Calendar layer shows a scheduled item's amount in its title even with Hide Amounts on; assess a narrow fix under B3.0B. B3.1 discovery, B4 subscription workflows and B5 household-wide reconciliation stay separately controlled. B2 was committed and pushed with owner/ChatGPT approval after the pre-commit corrections.
- **Not verified:** real phone, two physical devices, offline use, a real screen reader, measured contrast, a real older Ledger build (the older-build protection is a simulation of the B1 rule), the Calendar Week and Work-week views and .ics export contents. Browser checks (desktop, 375 px, light and dark, Hide Amounts, keyboard Tab and Escape, the Calendar month view) used the in-app pane with its own database and invented data.
- **Risks / carry-forward for review:** (1) a partial payment typed as paid outside Finance leaves the period open, so another reminder Task is made for it (explained in the preview); (2) a period moved or settled elsewhere can leave an older open Task standing for it: reported, never deleted; (3) look-back is 45 days for counts and 75 days for the bill page, older unrecorded months are simply not listed; (4) whole-record last-writer-wins still applies to an edit of one existing fact or the obligation (acknowledgements live on the obligation); (5) the "no deadline reminders" choice is a top-level `noDeadlineTasks` field that a B1 build keeps when it edits the bill (verified by simulation of the B1 rule, not on a real B1 build); (6) autopay wording says "not confirmed" because B2 reads no Finance data (B3 will say "not yet seen in Finance").

### Bills & Subscriptions B1 — obligation registry, schedule definition, basic lifecycle (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `3bcf3a3`, Pages run #69 success, verified at the start of B2; the "uncommitted" wording below was written before the commit and is SUPERSEDED; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B1-REPORT-2026-11-07.md`. **2,463 tests in 142 files** (baseline 2,265 in 133; 198 new in 9 new files). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` succeeds, `git diff --check` clean (line-ending notes only), schema still Dexie v15, no dependency, no backend, no change to `db.ts`, `merge.ts`, `backup.ts`, `src/types.ts`, Finance, Tasks, Calendar, Projects, Home & Assets or any Household Services behaviour.
- **Approval record (2026-11-07):** ChatGPT reviewed `docs/BILLS-SUBSCRIPTIONS-B0-PLAN-2026-11-07.md` Revision 2 and issued "B0 ARCHITECTURE APPROVED WITH IMPLEMENTATION SAFEGUARDS". The owner accepted the recommended defaults **A-1 to A-11** (A-1 new module; A-2 Finance scheduled items stay instructions; A-3 paid only with evidence, Finance status shown beside it; A-4 derived Calendar layer plus opt-in Tasks; A-5 tracking starts the day a bill is added; A-6 Services keeps service-agreement dates; A-7 Quick Add `bs`; A-8 loans excluded with "See Loans", optional modelled line off by default; A-9 no account numbers or credentials, Hide Amounts everywhere; A-10 phases B1 to B5; A-11 any obligation may go on the review list). **B1 only was authorized**; B2 and later are not. The B0 documentation was already committed (`8059e5f`, Pages run #68 success) when B1 began.
- **What B1 is:** a new `bills` module (route `#/bills`, `#/bills/list`, `#/bills/o/<id>`), records in `moduleRecords` (kind `obligation`; kind `cycle` is reserved for B2 and nothing writes one), a pure `expectedDates` reader, Overview (counts, expected recurring costs for 12 months split Known / Estimated / unknown, expected dates for 45 days, the owner's review list, things worth a look), All bills (Active / Paused / Ended / All, type and provider filters), a detail page, an Add/Edit form (three questions, "More details"), Pause / Resume / End / Reopen / Delete-only-when-empty with previews and a local guarded Undo, Search, Quick Add `bs`, record links, backup summary label.
- **B1.0 outcome (preferred path taken):** `semimonthly.ts` MOVED to `src/lib/semimonthly.ts` (code identical except the `dates` import path); `modules/services/semimonthly.ts` is now `export * from '../../lib/semimonthly'`. Services behaviour unchanged: all Services tests pass untouched, and a test proves the old path exports the very same function objects. Added `slotProblem` and `monthSlotDates` (one slot stepped by months). Oracle test: day 1 to 31 x every 1/2/3/6/12 months x three 31-day anchors x ten years including leap years equals `generateDates`; February anchors; 29 February annual.
- **Slot identity (B0 Revision 2 safeguards):** slots `m<YYYY-MM>`, `s<YYYY-MM>a|b`, `w<Monday>` are returned beside the nominal date and stored nowhere. Tested: a business-day adjustment, a changed day or a changed weekday never changes a slot; the actual due date is not an input to the reader; twice-monthly `a`/`b` follow rank, and over 3,431 valid rules and 2000 to 2130 the earlier date is always `a` (an exploration run, plus an in-suite sample). **A concrete B0 gap was found and handled, not improvised:** a "from now on" change that starts mid-month after that month's bill has passed makes two schedules claim one period (for example 12th to 20th on 15 December). `planScheduleChange` REFUSES it, naming the period and the nearest clean start date. A schedule that never applied to any date is simply replaced.
- **Invariants added:** expected is never spent (no paid, spent or payment field exists in any figure); an unknown amount is counted, never zero, and is in neither total; fixed is Known, variable is Estimated; annual and irregular amounts are never presented as monthly spending (the "averaged" line is the 12-month total divided by 12 and says so; on-demand bills are listed, not counted); loans are left out of every total; reading never writes; every write refuses when the record changed since the form or preview opened; unreadable stored values are shown as "Needs review" and written back byte for byte unless the owner changes that field; Undo of an add never deletes the shared Contact; permanent delete only for a bill with no history.
- **Evidence:** vitest including real Dexie, a real two-device `SyncEngine` and real backup export, checker and restore; 34 mutation checks (33 caught, one equivalent mutant: dead defensive code); source guards (no Finance write, no Task, Calendar, link or cycle write, no network, no storage, money only through the Hide-Amounts helpers); browser (invented data, the pane's own database) at desktop and 375 px, light and dark, Hide Amounts, validation, keyboard Tab and Escape, Quick Add, Pause then Undo, End then Reopen, delete refusal, schedule-change refusal and success. **NOT verified:** real phone, two physical devices, offline, real screen reader, measured contrast, a real older Ledger build (only simulated by unknown-module and unknown-field carry-through), the Quick Add Undo button and the empty-bill Delete success path in the browser (both unit-tested).
- **Not built (scope):** payments, Finance links, billing-period (`cycle`) records, conflict detection, status labels, Tasks, Calendar layer, Today card, due provider, contract, renewal and cancellation workflows, price-step editing, cross-module totals, the "Move to another billing period" operation, the Finance scheduled-item pointer screen, the Loan picker screen.
- **B2 prerequisite recorded:** B1's "replace" rule (an old schedule that never applied is simply replaced) looks only at derived dates. Once B2 stores a fact for a period before it is past, B2 MUST count periods that hold facts as "applied" in `planScheduleChange`, and must implement the controlled migration listed in report section 13 before any in-place schedule correction or any "Move to another billing period".

### G1 — Global Settings and Backup Integrity (COMMITTED AND DEPLOYED `fe2c673`, Pages run #63 success; not real-device tested)

Report: `docs/GLOBAL-SETTINGS-G1-REPORT-2026-11-06.md` (settings inventory, backup coverage matrix, restore semantics and limitations). **Where things live now:** app-wide Settings (`#/settings`): **General**, **Appearance** (colors, light/dark; the same `AppearanceCard`, same `localStorage` keys, no second store), **Data & Backups** (`#/settings/backup`), Sync devices, Calendar. Finance "More" keeps only Finance-specific options (decimal-point entry) and links to Settings; `#/ledger/backup` forwards to Settings.
- **Backup was already whole-database** (all 40 tables incl. every module's `moduleRecords`, one all-or-nothing restore transaction, table-coverage test). It is **full replacement**, not merge; plain JSON, **not encrypted** (an encrypted variant exists); attachments are metadata only (Ledger stores no file contents). G1 kept the format (data version 15, no migration) and added: optional `manifest` (record counts per table; files without it still restore), one read-transaction export, stricter pre-restore checks (usable ids, no repeated ids, unknown sections refused, future version refused first), encrypted restore through the same checker (+ preferences) and, after the final pre-commit correction, the SAME safety sequence as plain restore (decrypt and check, confirm, Pre-Import copy refusing on failure, one-transaction restore; one shared `restoreWithSafetyCopy`), a by-area summary (counts only), privacy warning text, plain restore explanation.
- **Sync interplay:** no tokens, passphrases or device state are in a backup; restore re-stamps records (and `touchAll` when sync is on) so the restored data wins; tested with the real `SyncEngine` on two devices. A restore on one synced device still replaces the others' data (documented in the UI).
- **Future modules:** shared-store records join backup automatically; a new table must be added to `exportAll`/`importAll`/`OPTIONAL_LISTS` and is caught by the coverage tests; process in `docs/module-hooks.md`.
- **Deferred:** optional encrypted-by-default backups (recommendation in the G1 report, section 18), attachment file storage, merge/per-module restore. Not done: real-device acceptance, keyboard-only and screen-reader pass, light-mode review.

### Household Services S0 — design (REVISION 2, FINAL-APPROVED; committed `e460c51`; Q1–Q9 accepted as option A)

Document: `docs/HOUSEHOLD-SERVICES-S0-PLAN-2026-11-06.md` (section 0 lists every Revision 2 change). Design only; no source change. **Revision 2 refinements (from ChatGPT's review):**
- **Scheduling authority:** arrangement owns the intended cadence (with `anchorDate`); the Task executes it. A reschedule moves one visit only; the standing cadence changes only via "Change schedule". At "Done, log it" (S2) Services sets the next due date from the cadence anchor via `generateDates` (no second engine). Drift (`scheduleHealth`) is detected and fixed only by explicit previewed choices. `ensureNextVisitTask` guarantees at most one open Task through pause/resume/replace; cross-device duplicates are detected, not auto-deleted. A timed visit is the Task with `dueTime`; no Calendar event in S1/S2. Regression lists in plan 6.10.
- **Visits:** completed, skipped and cancelled are distinct; cancelled visits can carry a fee (`chargeKind`) and a payment but are never service delivered; a Finance payment never proves work occurred.
- **Privacy:** no provider `accountNumber` in S1 (deferred); no codes, passwords or bank instructions in notes.
- **Household total:** `householdHomeSpend` specified in plan 7.6 (each linked payment once; whole supersedes parts; `recorded-as` pair rule; typed amounts never matched by value; shared payments never apportioned; never an allocation; no Finance change).
- **S1 scope:** arrangement-to-asset linking from the Services UI only; reverse Home & Assets UI is S4; S1 touches nothing under `src/modules/assets`.

Original model, still in force except where the above refines it:

- Provider = an ordinary shared **Contact** (no provider record; primary contact plus extra contacts; business-name snapshot). Two new `moduleRecords` kinds in module `services`: **arrangement** (standing relationship, cadence, expected cost, documents) and **visit** (created only when a visit is finished, skipped or cancelled; one-off visits need no arrangement). **No new table, schema stays 15.**
- Scheduling reuses Tasks: the arrangement stores the cadence; one linked recurring Task is "the next visit"; a Calendar event only for timed appointments. Skip/cancel are visit statuses; "Done, log it" is atomic with `completeTask` (H2 pattern). Seasonal ("April to October") needs a small optional wrapper in Services or a yearly-reminder fallback; Tasks and core recurrence are not changed.
- Money: H3-style read-only Finance references (`TxnRef`, `pieceKey`, live amount, split fingerprint); expected, quoted and charged amounts are labelled and **never counted as spent**; typed amounts only for "paid outside Finance"; one household-union helper de-duplicates by `pieceKey` (S5). Project money is shown beside, never added.
- Provider change = **successor arrangement**. History is preserved: no deletion-wins rule, no `merge.ts` change; arrangements with visits cannot be deleted; orphans repairable.
- Boundaries: maintenance of a thing -> Home & Assets (explicit prefilled "Record in Home & Assets", saves only on Save, `recorded-as` link); defined scope and budget -> Projects; subscriptions, bills and scheduled payments -> Bills & Subscriptions (Services never posts or schedules payments).
- Phases as planned in S0 (S1 to S4 are deployed; S5 is built and awaiting review): S1 arrangements + provider page + next-visit Task; S2 visits and Today card; S3 Finance; S4 cross-module workflows, renewals and a sixth Calendar layer (`..._106`); S5 overview, insights, acceptance.
- Q1–Q9 accepted as option A; S0 Revision 2 final-approved. S0 corrections applied at S1 start: 7.6 reconciliation is now claim-based (no simple identity; required S5 whole-versus-split cross-module test); no separate Calendar event for timed visits (one Task with a due time). Quick Add prefix `hs` is registered (the words `svc`, `a`, `b`, `p`, `t`, `e`, `$`, `service`, `repair` and the others stay taken).
