## 8. AI strategy — standing architectural direction

**Owner delegated to ChatGPT** the technical judgment of **where/how to introduce AI** and wants plain-language recommendations; **not** blanket authorization to send data externally or build an AI backend without a review.

- **H4:** do not expand H4 scope for AI; finish warranties/lifecycle correctly.
- **H5:** identify structured, permission-safe readers and potential insights; no chat integration required.
- **S0 (Household Services design) preserves this sequence:** deterministic insights only (S0 section 14), a name-only read-only reader modelled on H5's `loadOverview`, no AI interface in S1–S5, figures only from `spend()` and the household union, no phone/email/amount/URL/notes in any future AI context by default.
- **B5 AI-readiness (assessment, no interface built):** `loadHouseholdOverview` and the pure `recordedAcrossModules`, `householdCommitments` and `householdReview` are a small, read-only, deterministic context surface for a future "Ask Ledger": every figure is a sum of listed pieces, every piece carries its associations and its reason, text the data layer writes never contains an amount, and nothing is written. A model should only interpret these results; it must not recalculate them, and no name, amount, provider note or account detail should reach one without the explicit controls above.
- **Household Services and Bills/Subscriptions:** add useful deterministic insights (maintenance due, upcoming payments, renewals, cost changes) without unnecessarily paying for an LLM. Label these "insights" rather than pretending they require AI.
- **First “Ask Ledger” conversational capability** likely after Services and Bills/Subscriptions reach stable form, earlier if justified. Let user ask household questions; resolve money/date facts with Ledger's established readers/calculation engine, using a model only to interpret/summarize.
- **Actions later:** proposals for task creation, categorization, service logging, etc. should show exact preview and require user confirmation. Deletion, purchases, high-stakes actions require stronger safeguards.
- **Proactive assistance** last: weekly briefings, reminders and permitted monitoring, with bounded scope, user controls, no surprise writes.
- **Security:** local-first, minimal selected data sent only with explicit controls. **Do not embed external AI API credentials in browser/client code.** Evaluate a small authenticated proxy/backend or secure alternative before external-model use, with a specific architectural approval covering privacy, operating cost, prompt injection and per-action permissions. No automatic sharing of whole household backups with a model.
- All AI findings are aids, not authoritative ledger postings or legal/tax/medical claims.

- **Household Services design risks (S0 section 17):** seasonal schedules unsupported by Tasks; rescheduling a weekly Task may shift later weekdays and every-other-week parity (S1 RESOLVED the S1 part: weekly cadences always carry an explicit weekday, a one-time Task date change leaves the arrangement and later dates unchanged, tested; the completed-visit progression, "Done, log it" and next-date alignment are S2 and still untested); the Task editor preserving the Services markers is CONFIRMED (test and browser); no Contacts screen and no company/archive field on Contact; two devices finishing the same repeating Task create two "next" Tasks (existing Tasks behaviour); one payment cannot be apportioned across visits; typed amounts have no de-duplication key; `spend()` and `txnRef.ts` need a small test-protected extraction in S3. **S2 update:** the completed-visit progression is now built and tested (cadence-anchored next date, early/late, moved-once, repeat-after); remaining S2 risks: two devices that both log one Task keep both visits (flagged, no resolution workflow) and each makes a next Task (flagged as duplicate-open); a later write on another device can reopen a logged Task (the visit survives and a second Done is refused, tested); Tasks finished elsewhere stay "not yet logged" until the owner logs them (no dismiss); an unrecognised stored visit status is shown as "Needs review" until the owner chooses a real outcome; the Undo guard relies on millisecond `updatedAt` and S1 lifecycle Undos are unguarded; an operation id lasts only as long as the open form or preview; seasonal scheduling is deferred.
- **S2.1 carry-forward:** Home & Assets real-device acceptance and Household Services real-device acceptance are both still NOT DONE. Remaining Undo limits: a refusal gives no automatic way back (edit by hand); the guard is deliberately conservative (any re-save of a touched record, even with the same content, refuses; any new link on a touched record refuses); a change to a record Undo never touched (for example the arrangement after a Tasks-only fix) does not block it; sync still resolves ties and whole-record conflicts by last-writer-wins (unchanged). Seasonal scheduling and duplicate-visit resolution remain deferred; S3 is the next functional phase.

Not browser-checked: light mode of the new sections at 375 px, the Move and Contract dialogs at 375 px, a keyboard Tab walk, a screen reader. The handoff has no Undo toast (delete the record in Home & Assets). **Test-hygiene lesson from S3:** a test must never compare a stored real-clock value to a fixed calendar day (run #64 failed at 04:12 UTC on Nov 7). **Today error (FIXED in the S4 corrections):** an account with no opening date used to crash the budget and the Today attention card; fixed with two guards (nothing invented). Other screens may still show odd text for such an account; restore does not validate account fields.

Not re-run in the browser in S5: creating or editing an arrangement, saving a visit, the asset handoff, Project linking, the Calendar view. Real screen reader and measured contrast not done. The household union covers only these two modules and includes asset purchases; a linked typed job is dated by the visit; typed amounts are left out when the job has any Finance reference even a stale one (S0 7.6). Today does not show the three Overview-only conditions (stale Finance links, duplicate handoffs, typed-amount notes). Whole-record last-writer-wins unchanged.
- **B3 carry-forward (Bills & Subscriptions Finance links, B3.0A, B3.0B):** real-device, two-device, offline, real older build, real screen reader and measured-contrast acceptance are all still NOT DONE (simulations use the real `SyncEngine` and the in-app browser pane only). Known limits: one bill paid partly in Finance and partly in cash cannot be represented (the one-source rule; owner decision O-1); the Finance rows are read just BEFORE a write transaction opens, so a Finance change in that instant leaves the planned reminders one step stale until the next save; a stale link keeps Finance as the source, so a typed amount never stands in for it silently; removing a payment makes one reminder for the reopened period while a later reminder already made is reported, not deleted; "marked paid in full, Finance shows less" keeps the owner's statement and states the difference (O-4); a `payment-shared` piece (one payment on several periods) is flagged with "leave as is" only, the owner removes the wrong link by hand; the .ics export keeps amounts when Hide Amounts is on (O-2) and Home "Coming up" still shows Finance amounts (O-3); the two new preferences are per-device and not in backups (`services-upcoming-days`, `calendar-hidden-layers`); hiding one calendar by hand makes later new calendars start off (O-5); Finance's pending, cleared and reconciled are owner marks, never bank settlement; real Finance transactions are read live, so a restored backup shows a link as "missing" until the transaction exists on that device. Standing invariants B3 relies on and must not break: Finance owns transactions and Bills never writes one; one money source per period; each Finance piece counted once by `pieceKey`; typed amounts never matched by value; the planner always receives the live Finance lookup; reads write nothing.
- **B3.1 carry-forward (Bills & Subscriptions discovery, suggestions, Scheduled link):** real-device, two-device, offline, real older build, real screen reader, real Tab walk and screenshot review are NOT done (simulations use the real `SyncEngine` and the in-app browser pane, DOM-level, only). Known limits: a device still on the B3 build shows a `suggestion-reject` under Needs review as an unreadable record until it updates; discovery needs at least three payments (or two nearly identical, shown only as "Needs more information") at weekly, biweekly, monthly, quarterly or yearly spacing, merges accounts, reads a split at its whole amount, and splits a payee whose bank text changes; payee matching is a word heuristic (two small word lists in `payeeMatch.ts`); suggestions look at a window around the due date and show at most three; the Finance panel compares a bill with its rule only when both use the same cadence; Household Services contract markers are not coordinated; B3's backup test has a vacuous "leaks no names" assertion (noted, not changed). Owner decisions O-6 to O-12 are in the B3.1 report section 21.
- **B0 carry-forward (Bills & Subscriptions, proposed):** real-device acceptance for Home & Assets and Household Services is still NOT DONE and is inherited by Bills' Calendar and Task behaviour. Risks recorded in the plan (section 16): the same bill tracked as a Finance scheduled item and a Bills obligation (never summed; link or ignore); wrongly confirmed matches (never automatic, one-tap unlink); autopay read as paid (state wording and grace period); whole-record last-writer-wins on obligation-level price steps, schedule revisions and ends (facts and payment links are not exposed to it in Revision 2); the optional shared slot-helper move (with a copy-and-sweep fallback; the Undo guard is not extracted); the golden-master gate before any B5 cross-module figure; Finance's `dueInstances` listing every missed date from a rule's start (noted, not changed, and not copied into Bills). Standing decisions that B0 relies on and must not break: Finance owns payments; one source per cost; each Finance piece counted once by `pieceKey`; typed amounts never matched by value; Calendar shows a rolling 12 months derived, never stored, never pre-created Tasks; Contacts for providers; no account numbers or credentials.
- **B1 carry-forward (Bills & Subscriptions):** real-device acceptance for Home & Assets, Household Services and now Bills is still NOT DONE. Known limits: only a first price is editable (the list shape is final for B4); a schedule change from a mid-month date after that month's bill is refused rather than guessed (B2 must count periods that hold facts as applied before it replaces a schedule); twice-monthly slots `a`/`b` are positional, so an in-place correction that changes their meaning must stay a dated revision once facts exist; whole-record last-writer-wins can supersede one of two concurrent edits of one bill (the loser is saved as an "Edits that lost a tie" notice, device-local and capped at 50); the Undo guard is deliberately conservative (any change to the touched row refuses); no cycle records, orphan repair or conflict detection yet (B2); the Loan picker and Finance scheduled-item pointer have no screen yet (fields are kept); a restore replaces everything, as today.
- Avoid building duplicate financial engines, calendar events, task recurrence or vendor lists.
- Keep backlog and handoff accurate after **commit/deploy**, not merely after implementation reports. Never mark draft/plan features as built.

## 10. Standing Operating Procedure — maintaining THIS handoff

**Target file:** `docs/CHATGPT-HANDOFF.md` in the user's local repo. **Update owner:** Claude working locally, or another tool with actual repo write access. ChatGPT can compose a replacement and review it; **ChatGPT cannot continuously or automatically modify the user's Windows directory from an ordinary chat**. Do not claim to have done so. The repository instruction in `CLAUDE.md` should direct Claude to update it routinely.

### When to update (mandatory checkpoints)

- After **every** approved architecture/design decision, changed user preference, phase boundary, material blocker, resolved issue, or change to long-term roadmap.
- At the **end of every Claude implementation/report batch**, update current phase status and tests, but mark **uncommitted** until explicitly committed.
- **After each commit/push and Actions completion**, refresh actual SHA, deployment state, newest test baseline and next step; don't leave “not pushed” status once deployed.
- Before handing off to a new ChatGPT chat, ensure the document reflects actual `main`, in-flight uncommitted work, current pending decision and exact next action.

### Maintenance procedure (for Claude/local agent)

1. Read `CLAUDE.md`, current handoff, latest backlog, recent phase report and `git status`/`git log`; when possible check deployment status. Verify facts, including whether files are local-only.
2. Update **Section 1** (snapshot/next action/test baseline/SHA), **Section 6** (phase delivery status), **Section 7** (roadmap), **Section 8** (AI direction), **Section 9** (open risks), and record material newly approved decisions in the appropriate section. Add a short dated update line in Section 11. Keep invariant sections stable unless consciously changed.
3. Preserve accurate architectural detail while pruning duplicate wording; link to full reports rather than copying long logs. Distinguish `planned`, `implemented but uncommitted`, `committed`, `deployed`, `accepted` and `not verified`.
4. Never embed actual data or secrets; audit the diff for accidental PII. Maintain plain Markdown, relative paths and public-repo compatibility.
5. Update `docs/BACKLOG.md` consistently; resolve contradictory top statuses. Neither file overrides tested code/Git. Ensure the handoff accurately reflects decisions made in ChatGPT **when they are explicitly supplied to Claude**.
6. **Include the handoff in the same reviewed change set as the relevant phase**; do not create a separate commit/push without authorization. If a deployment SHA isn't known until after push, update it in the next authorized documentation change or keep a labeled “verification pending” entry; avoid an unauthorized follow-up push solely to stamp a SHA.
7. In the final implementation report, include: `Handoff updated: yes/no`, what sections changed, outstanding items, and any difference between local docs, committed GitHub and deployment.
8. If no repo-write tool is available, prepare a complete Markdown replacement or patch and ask the owner to have Claude apply it. Do not claim the local file has changed.

### For ChatGPT in each future conversation

- Read the latest uploaded/accessible `docs/CHATGPT-HANDOFF.md` at the start of Ledger work. Cross-check current Git status and backlog where accessible. Do not infer access to the user's uncommitted Windows files.
- During ordinary architectural reviews, recommend exact handoff updates to include in Claude prompts. ChatGPT cannot guarantee updates in conversations it is not running.
- When a phase reaches implementation/review, explicitly require handoff maintenance in the Claude prompt and check the report says it was done.
- If user asks to “update the handoff,” produce the revised artifact or an actionable repo-local Claude prompt **now**, rather than promising invisible background edits.

Did not switch the default appearance (Classic is stored as nothing; report section 5).
